TL;DR: 本研究在搜救模拟环境中对比了有无LLM辅助的人类表现，发现LLM指导提升了任务效率但未增加总营救人数，眼动数据揭示了注意力-指导权衡，且专家与新手展现出不同的交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统（特别是LLM）在实时复杂任务中如何影响人类认知和行为，以及这种影响如何随专业水平变化，从而理解人-AI协同的有效性机制。

Method: 在模拟搜救环境中设置三种条件（两种LLM引导条件与无LLM基线），结合任务绩效指标、眼动追踪（注意力分配）和行为分析（规划与决策结构）进行多层面比较。

Result: LLM引导提升了任务效率（更高奖励和每步营救数），但未增加总营救人数；眼动数据显示注意力向聊天界面转移且瞳孔大小变异性增大；专家保持环境扫描的"验证循环"，新手则表现出被动AI依赖。

Conclusion: LLM中介的团队效能取决于操作者交叉验证AI指导与地面实况以维持情境意识的能力。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming (HAT) increasingly involves AI systems that provide real-time, context-aware guidance in complex tasks. While such systems can improve performance, their effectiveness depends on how they shape human cognition and behavior. In particular, AI assistance can introduce cognitive demands and influence attention, planning, and interaction with the task environment, with effects that can vary across levels of expertise. This work investigates these mechanisms in a simulated search and rescue (SAR) environment. We compare human performance under two LLM (Large Language Model)-guided conditions and a no-LLM baseline, and analyze interaction at multiple levels, including task performance, eye-tracking measures, and planning behavior. Eye tracking provides fine-grained insight into attention allocation and interaction with AI guidance, while behavioral measures capture how users structure and adapt their decisions over time. Results indicate that LLM guidance enhanced task efficiency (higher rewards and victims-per-step) but did not increase total victims saved. Eye-tracking data revealed an attention-guidance trade-off, with visual resources shifting to the chat interface alongside increased pupil size variability. Expertise moderated this effect: novices exhibited passive AI reliance, whereas experts maintained a "verification loop" through persistent environmental scanning. These findings suggest that LLM-mediated teaming efficacy depends on the operator's ability to cross-reference AI guidance with ground truth to maintain situational awareness.

</details>


### [190] [Code as Anchor, Memory and Metaphor as Support: Learner Experiences with Multi-View Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19570)
*Naaz Sibia,Jessica Wen,Amber Richardson,Yashika Jain,Khushi Malik,Bogdan Simion,Carolina Nobre,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Andrew Petersen,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过眼动追踪和访谈研究发现，学生在编程学习中尽管有可视化辅助，仍将近一半时间花在代码上；缺乏经验的学生更依赖代码、回避隐喻视图；选择性地参与可视化工具受制于三个因素：自主权（控制认知努力）、表征适配性（相同设计因人而异）、合法性（隐喻工具被视为不够严谨）。研究提出可视化工具应注重情感和社会因素，如定位为验证工具、提供可调抽象层级、提升学科合法性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序可视化被广泛用于支持编程初学者，但学生常常忽视或抗拒这些精心设计的可视化脚手架。现有关于多重外部表征(MERs)的研究提供了协调视图的认知设计原则，但对什么因素塑造了学习者对可用表征的参与度知之甚少。

Method: 对19名已完成CS1和CS2的本科生进行被试内研究，采用多表征探测工具（同步代码、内存和隐喻视图）及Python Tutor，通过边想边说任务、反思性访谈和基于网络摄像头的注视追踪收集数据。

Result: 注视分析显示学生近一半时间聚焦于代码，尽管有可视化脚手架可用。缺乏经验的学生更依赖代码，几乎不参与隐喻视图。访谈发现三个影响选择性参与的因素：自主权（学生寻求对认知努力的控制而非简单减少）、表征适配性（相同设计有人觉得有用有人觉得 overwhelmed）、合法性（部分学生认为隐喻脚手架过于幼稚或不够严谨，不适合大学水平学习）。

Conclusion: 编程教育中的多表征工具除了注重认知设计外，还需关注情感和社会因素。实际建议包括：将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可切换的抽象层级、以及框架化工具以传递学科合法性。这些发现有助于解释为什么认知上合理的可视化工具可能无法吸引它们本应帮助的学生。

Abstract: Program visualizations are widely used to support novice programmers, yet students often ignore or resist well-designed visual scaffolds. Research on multiple external representations (MERs) offers cognitive design principles for coordinating views, but less is known about what shapes learners' engagement with available representations.
  We conducted a within-subjects study with 19 undergraduates who had completed CS1 and CS2. Students completed think-aloud tasks, reflective interviews, and webcam-based gaze tracking while using a multi-representational probe with synchronized code, memory, and metaphor views, and Python Tutor, across scope, while loops, and linked lists.
  Gaze analysis showed that students spent nearly half their time focused on code despite available visual scaffolds. Students without prior experience anchored even more heavily in code and engaged minimally with metaphor views. Interviews identified three factors shaping selective engagement: agency, as students sought control over cognitive effort rather than simply having it reduced; representational fit, as identical designs differed in whether they felt helpful or overwhelming; and legitimacy, as some students avoided metaphorical scaffolds they perceived as childish or insufficiently rigorous for university-level work.
  These findings suggest that multi-representational tools in computing education require attention to affective and social factors alongside cognitive design. Practical considerations include positioning visualizations as verification instruments, offering toggleable abstraction levels, and framing tools to signal disciplinary legitimacy. More broadly, the themes help explain why cognitively sound visualization tools may fail to engage the students they are designed to help.

</details>


### [191] [Building Drift: Documenting On-Site Construction Adaptations Across Material Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19609)
*Ritik Batra,Martin Tamke,Tom Svilans,Jan Hüls,Amritansh Kwatra,Steven J. Jackson,Thijs Roumen,Mette Ramsgaard Thomsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"建筑漂移"概念，描述回收材料建筑中物理状态与数字模型的集体偏差，通过ReShelter案例建立分类体系（场地照料、适配搜寻、材料解读、测量标记、社区协调），并开发了基于视频和3D高斯泼溅的文档工具Pentimento，以支持回收材料建筑中的现场即兴创作和可持续循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回收材料在建筑中的使用带来不可预测性，需要现场即兴调整，但缺乏有效的文档记录方式，导致协作方、评估方和后续使用者无法获取必要信息来继续评估和再利用材料，阻碍了建筑行业的循环经济发展。

Method: 通过ReShelter（森林中建造的回收木材亭）案例研究，建立"建筑漂移"分类体系；开发Pentimento工具，利用视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术，从空间、时间和语义三个维度呈现现场适应性调整与设计模型的关系。

Result: 建立了包含五个类别的建筑漂移分类体系：场地照料、适配搜寻、材料解读、测量标记、社区协调。Pentimento工具使各利益相关方能够导航材料历史，降低材料再利用的障碍。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为支持回收材料建筑中不可或缺的现场即兴创作的计算工具开辟了路径，促进了更可持续的回收、修复和再利用循环。

Abstract: In a circular economy for construction, reclaimed materials carry prior lives of use and go on to have post-lives in future buildings. Yet working with such materials introduces unpredictability that requires on-site improvisation, making their reuse challenging to document and scale across building lifetimes. Without documentation, the on-site adaptations that make construction with reclaimed materials possible leave collaborators, evaluators, and inheritors without the information they need to continue, assess, and reuse materials. We call the collective deviation of the physical state from the digital model through these adaptations "building drift." Through a case study, ReShelter, a reclaimed timber pavilion constructed in the forest, we develop a taxonomy for building drift that characterizes the collective deviation across building lifetimes: Tending the Site, Foraging for Fit, Interpreting the Material, Marking Measurements, and Coordinating Across Communities.
  To put our taxonomy for building drift into practice, we present Pentimento, a documentation tool that leverages video documentation and 3D Gaussian Splatting to spatially, temporally, and semantically represent on-site adaptations in relation to the designed model. Pentimento enables each stakeholder to navigate material histories in ways that reduce barriers to material reuse. Together, these contributions open pathways towards computational tools that support the on-site improvisation essential to construction with reclaimed materials, enabling more sustainable cycles of recovery, repair, and reuse.

</details>


### [192] [Syndesmoscope: The Power of Invariant Plots\\Linked to Traditional Network Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19689)
*Matt Oddo,Indira Sowy,Stephen Kobourov,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Syndesmoscope，一个通过并置多种不变图布局（力导向图、密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率、谱二分）以及新提出的 kSnakes 密度分解图来探索网络的交互式系统，支持跳跃式高亮和跳房子式遍历两种交互方式，能够在单一视图无法揭示的网络模式中提供洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络可视化（节点-链接图、邻接矩阵）的视觉模式高度依赖布局或序列化算法，而不变图（invariant plots）能稳定呈现相同拓扑的同一视觉模式，但此前未被充分探索和集成到可视化系统中。

Method: 设计 Syndesmoscope 系统，并置四种可解释的几何布局（密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率、谱二分）与传统力导向视图，并引入基于密度分解的新不变图 kSnakes。支持两种交互：leapfrogging（跨不同可解释视觉模式的联动高亮）和 hopscotching（基于跳数的拓扑遍历选择扩展）。

Result: 在 72 个多样化网络的语料库上通过使用场景展示了该系统如何揭示单一视图无法获得的网络模式。

Conclusion: 不变图的并置与特定交互设计能有效揭示传统单视图难以发现的网络结构特征。

Abstract: Traditional network representations, such as node-link views and adjacency matrices, can show dramatically different visual patterns, depending on the underlying layout or seriation algorithm. In contrast, invariant plots consistently surface the same visual pattern for the same input topology; yet researchers have underexplored them and have not integrated them into visualization systems. We present Syndesmoscope, an interactive system for network exploration that juxtaposes multiple views of the same network. Panes show a familiar a force-directed view alongside three panes with interpretable geometric layouts based on graph-theoretic properties: dense-sparse gradient, geodesic eccentricity, and spectral bisection. As a secondary contribution, we introduce kSnakes, a new invariant plot based on density decomposition. Syndesmoscope supports two key interactions: leapfrogging, or linked highlighting between different and interpretable visual patterns; and hopscotching, or hop-based traversal that extends data selections through the underlying topology. Through usage scenarios across a corpus of 72 diverse networks, we demonstrate how these interactions reveal network patterns inaccessible through any single view alone. Live demo available at https://syndesmoscope.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [193] [Vibe Coding for Visualization Implementation: An Empirical Study of Practices and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19703)
*Zhengyu Sun,Xiaolin Wen,Fengjie Wang,Can Liu,Yi Lai,Christophe Hurter,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过16名参与者的实证研究，探究了用户如何使用AI驱动的"vibe coding"工具进行数据可视化实现，揭示了用户在提示、评估和迭代过程中的多样化实践与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI驱动的自然语言交互工具降低了数据可视化的门槛，但可视化实现需要用户意图与视觉表示之间的精确对齐，这与通用软件开发有所不同，目前缺乏对用户如何使用这类工具进行可视化的系统理解。

Method: 招募16名不同专业水平的参与者，让他们使用vibe coding工具完成两个可视化任务，并进行半结构化访谈，通过实证研究分析用户行为。

Result: 揭示了用户在提示(prompting)、评估(evaluation)和迭代(iteration)过程中的多样化实践方式，以及在此过程中遇到的各种挑战。

Conclusion: 用户在使用vibe coding工具进行可视化实现时采用了多样化的策略，但面临着意图与视觉表示对齐的独特挑战，表明可视化场景对AI交互工具有特定的设计需求。

Abstract: Data visualization is essential for data analysis and communication, yet creating expressive visualizations remains labor-intensive. Recent AI-driven ``vibe coding'' tools enable users to generate visualizations through natural language interaction, lowering the barrier to entry. However, visualization implementation requires precise alignment between user intent and visual representation, which may differ from general software development practices. We present an empirical study with 16 participants of varying expertise to examine how users employ vibe coding tools for visualization implementation. Participants completed two visualization tasks and a semi-structured interview. Our findings characterize the diverse practices users adopt across prompting, evaluation, and iteration, and surface the challenges they encounter throughout the process.

</details>


### [194] [Designing for Interconnected Islamic Learning: A Qualitative Study of Muslim Women's Experiences with Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19745)
*Ishrat Jahan Easha,Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Araf Mohammad Mahbub,Fairoze Bint Abu Hassan,Zunaid Aslam,Yemin Sajid,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对五名穆斯林女性的半结构化访谈，本文探讨了伊斯兰学习中《古兰经》、圣训和先知传记因数字工具分离导致的HCI问题，提出“分层语境性”概念，强调在数字阅读中平衡语境扩展与解释可信度、 devotional flow 及跨设备连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰学习通常需要同时阅读《古兰经》、圣训（Hadith）和先知传记（Seerah），但现有数字工具将这些资源分散在不同的应用、屏幕和搜索路径中，破坏了学习的整体性和流畅性。本研究从人机交互（HCI）视角出发，旨在理解和解决这一数字学习中的语境断裂问题。

Method: 从一个在线伊斯兰学习社区招募五名穆斯林女性参与半结构化访谈，通过质性分析提炼主题。理论框架整合了性别化的数字宗教（gendered digital religion）、认知信任（epistemic trust）和无缝学习（seamless learning）三个视角。

Result: 识别出五个主题：语境理解（contextual understanding）、真实性（authenticity）、界面杂乱（interface clutter）、学习模式（study modes）和引导功能（guidance features）。参与者在阅读时希望获得《古兰经》-圣训-先知传记的上下文关联，但要求这种扩展必须可信、可选且不打断阅读流。

Conclusion: 提出“分层语境性”（layered contextuality）这一HCI概念，认为数字工具中的语境扩展需要在解释可信度（interpretive accountability）、 devotional flow 和跨设备与不同学习强度的连续性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Islamic learning often depends on reading the Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah together, yet digital tools typically separate these sources across apps, screens, and search pathways. We examine this as a human-computer interaction problem through five semi-structured interviews with Muslim women recruited from an online Islamic learning community. Participants described a recurring tension: they wanted Qur'an-Hadith-Seerah context at the point of reading, but only when contextual expansion remained trustworthy, optional, and did not interrupt reading. Interpreting the interviews through gendered digital religion, epistemic trust, and seamless learning, we identify five themes concerning contextual understanding, authenticity, interface clutter, study modes, and guidance features. We introduce layered contextuality as an HCI account of this domain: contextual expansion must be balanced with interpretive accountability, devotional flow, and continuity across devices and study intensities.

</details>


### [195] [MemGUI-Agent: An End-to-End Long-Horizon Mobile GUI Agent with Proactive Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19926)
*Guangyi Liu,Gao Wu,Congxiao Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Liang Liu,Mading Li,Qi Zhang,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出MemGUI-Agent，一种基于"上下文即动作"(ConAct)范式的端到端长时移动GUI智能体，通过将上下文管理建模为与UI操作同等的首要动作，维持紧凑的结构化上下文，解决ReAct提示在长任务中的提示爆炸和关键信息稀释问题。在8B参数规模上，训练于MemGUI-3K数据集（2956条轨迹，含完整ConAct标注）的MemGUI-8B-SFT在MemGUI-Bench上达到最优8B开放数据性能，并能泛化到分布外的MobileWorld基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的移动GUI智能体在短时任务上表现良好，但在需要跨多步和跨应用保留中间事实的长时任务中不可靠。根本原因是ReAct风格的提示方式被动累积每步记录，导致提示爆炸和关键跨应用事实的稀释。

Method: 提出Context-as-Action (ConAct)范式，将上下文管理建模为与UI操作同等地位的首要动作，由同一策略输出。维护三个结构化上下文字段：折叠动作历史、折叠UI状态和近期步骤记录。构建含2956条完整ConAct标注轨迹的MemGUI-3K数据集进行监督训练。

Result: MemGUI-8B-SFT（8B参数）在MemGUI-Bench上达到最优8B开放数据性能，并泛化到分布外的MobileWorld基准。

Conclusion: 将上下文管理作为可学习的首要动作能显著提升移动GUI智能体在长时任务中的可靠性，且该方法在不同模型规模下均可学习并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress on short-horizon tasks, yet remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks that require retaining intermediate facts across many steps and app transitions. We attribute this limitation to ReAct-style prompting, which passively accumulates per-step records, leading to prompt explosion and dilution of critical cross-app facts. To address this, we introduce MemGUI-Agent, an end-to-end long-horizon mobile GUI agent with proactive context management. MemGUI-Agent is built on Context-as-Action (ConAct), which casts context management as first-class actions emitted by the same policy that selects UI actions. Instead of passively appending history, ConAct maintains three structured context fields: folded action history, folded UI state, and recent step record, preserving critical UI facts while keeping context compact. To make proactive context management learnable across model scales, we construct MemGUI-3K, a 2,956-trajectory dataset with full ConAct annotations for supervised training and offline analysis. Training an 8B model on MemGUI-3K produces MemGUI-8B-SFT, an 8B MemGUI-Agent that achieves the best open-data 8B performance on MemGUI-Bench and generalizes to the out-of-distribution MobileWorld benchmark. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://memgui-agent.github.io/.

</details>


### [196] [MobileForge: Annotation-Free Adaptation for Mobile GUI Agents with Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19930)
*Guangyi Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Gao Wu,Yiwen Yin,Mading Li,Liang Liu,Congxiao Liu,Zhang Qi,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MobileForge是一个无需人工标注的移动GUI智能体适配系统，通过MobileGym环境与HiFPO分层反馈优化策略，使Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上达到67.2% Pass@3，接近闭源模型，而ForgeOwl-8B达到77.6% Pass@3，建立最强开源移动GUI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的移动GUI智能体在适配真实目标应用时成本高昂，因为移动应用数量众多、更新频繁，且依赖人工编写的任务、演示或奖励标签难以覆盖。现有免标注方法缺乏统一框架来衔接目标应用探索、课程挖掘、轨迹执行和反馈，且策略优化依赖孤立轨迹和粗糙奖励，难以转化为可靠的改进信号。

Method: 提出MobileForge系统，包含两个核心组件：1) MobileGym——将任务生成和轨迹评估锚定在真实移动应用交互中；2) 分层反馈引导策略优化(HiFPO)——将轨迹结果、步骤级过程反馈和修正提示转化为提示上下文化的步骤级GRPO更新。

Result: MobileForge适配的Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上达到67.2% Pass@3，接近闭源GUI专用模型GUI-Owl-1.5-8B的69.0%；ForgeOwl-8B达到77.6% Pass@3，在域外MobileWorld GUI-only分割上达到41.0%成功率，建立最强开源移动GUI智能体。

Conclusion: 证明了完全无需人工标注的自动适配策略可以在移动GUI智能体任务上达到与闭源、专用模型相媲美甚至超越的性能，展示了免标注适配范式的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress in UI understanding and action execution, but adapting them to real target apps remains costly because mobile apps are numerous, frequently updated, and hard to cover with human-written tasks, demonstrations, or reward labels. Existing annotation-free GUI learning reduces manual supervision, yet lacks a unified substrate connecting target-app exploration, curriculum mining, rollout execution, and feedback, while policy optimization often relies on isolated rollouts and coarse rewards that are hard to convert into reliable improvement signals. We present MobileForge, an annotation-free adaptation system for mobile GUI agents. MobileForge consists of MobileGym, which grounds task generation and rollout evaluation in real mobile app interaction, and Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization (HiFPO), which turns trajectory outcomes, step-level process feedback, and corrective hints into hint-contextualized step-level GRPO updates. Using only automatically generated annotation-free adaptation data, MobileForge adapts Qwen3-VL-8B to 67.2% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld, close to the closed-data GUI-specialized GUI-Owl-1.5-8B base model at 69.0%. The MobileForge-adapted ForgeOwl-8B further reaches 77.6% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld and 41.0% success on the out-of-domain MobileWorld GUI-only split, establishing the strongest open-data mobile GUI agent in our evaluation. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://mobile-forge.github.io/.

</details>


### [197] [DataMagic: Transforming Tabular Data into Data Insight Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20388)
*Yupeng Xie,Chen Ma,Zhenyang Wang,Liangwei Wang,Jiayi Zhu,Chuxuan Zeng,Zhouan Shen,Boyan Li,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DataMagic是一个端到端交互系统，可将原始表格数据和自然语言查询自动转化为叙事性数据洞察视频，通过声明式规范DVSpec保证数据保真度，并采用"先生成后编排"的多智能体架构解决设计空间组合爆炸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量数据视频制作需要数据分析、叙事设计和视频制作等多领域专业知识，现有方法（静态可视化工具、创作工具、像素级视频生成模型）在叙事逻辑、动画支持、数据保真度等方面存在不足。

Method: 提出声明式规范DVSpec，通过数据驱动语义引用将视觉和动画元素绑定到数据字段；采用"先生成后编排"多智能体架构，并行生成候选场景再全局优化叙事连贯性；支持三种交互模式和基于来源的数据问答。

Result: 在109个真实世界样本上进行了评估，验证了DataMagic系统的有效性。

Conclusion: DataMagic通过端到端自动化流程降低了数据视频制作门槛，同时保证了数据保真度和叙事质量，并将单向视频转化为可探索的交互式数据界面。

Abstract: Data videos integrate dynamic charts, voice narration, and synchronized animations to communicate data insights as temporal narratives, making them an effective medium for improving data consumption efficiency in the data management lifecycle. However, producing high-quality data videos requires expertise spanning data analysis, narrative design, and video production. Existing approaches fall short: static visualization tools (e.g., BI dashboards) lack narrative logic and animation; authoring tools require users to pre-prepare visualizations rather than working from raw data; pixel-level video generation models cannot guarantee data fidelity or provenance. We demonstrate DataMagic, an end-to-end interactive system that transforms raw tabular data and natural language queries into narrative data-insight videos. To ensure data fidelity, DataMagic introduces the declarative specification DVSpec, which binds visual and animation elements to underlying data fields through data-driven semantic references. To address the combinatorial explosion of the design space, DataMagic adopts a Generate-then-Orchestrate multi-agent architecture that generates candidate scenes in parallel and then optimizes narrative coherence through global orchestration. Leveraging DVSpec's decoupling of logic and rendering, the system further supports three interaction modes and structured provenance-based data Q&A, transforming one-way videos into explorable interactive data interfaces. Evaluation on 109 real-world samples validates the effectiveness of the DataMagic. Homepage: https://datamagic-home.github.io/

</details>


### [198] [AI Conversational Interviewing: Scaling Up Semi-Structured and In-depth Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20064)
*Alexander Wuttke,Max Melchior Lang,Christopher Klamm,Quirin Würschinger,Frauke Kreuter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AI对话式访谈方法，通过在571名受访者中结合AI引导的开放访谈与标准化调查，证明该方法能捕捉标准化问卷无法获取的深层公众意见（如移民问题的心理模型差异），且受访者评价不逊于传统调查，为大规模开放型意见收集提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公共舆论研究在深度与广度之间存在权衡：标准化调查可大规模测量但限制了受访者表达范围，而开放式访谈虽有深度但依赖训练有素的人类访谈员难以规模化。本研究旨在引入AI对话式访谈作为规模化收集开放式舆论数据的方法。

Method: 在Prolific和Payback Panel招募571名受访者，结合AI引导的对话式访谈与标准化调查，实验比较语音、文本和自由选择三种访谈模式，并通过受访者自评评估方法的可行性。

Result: AI对话式访谈能捕捉标准化问卷无法获得的深层思考和推理（如态度相似但心理模型不同的亚群体）；完成访谈的受访者对各模式的AI访谈评价不低于标准化调查，但完成率因条件而异。

Conclusion: AI对话式访谈是社会科学工具库中可行且有价值的补充方法，通过发布开放数据和开源管线，有助于推动利用人工智能扩展舆论测量方法的研究。

Abstract: Public opinion research has long faced a trade-off between depth and scale: standardized surveys enable large-scale measurement but restrict respondents to researcher-defined categories, obscuring the diversity of unexpected considerations that underlie public sentiment. More conversational interviews provide richer insights through open-ended probing, but their reliance on trained human interviewers has kept them difficult to scale. This study introduces AI Conversational Interviewing as a method for collecting open-ended public opinion data at scale, pursuing three objectives: to demonstrate the analytical value of conversational text data for questions beyond the reach of closed-ended items; to assess the method's practical viability through participants' own evaluations; and to inform implementation by experimentally comparing voice-based, chat-based, and free-choice interview modes. We conducted a study combining an AI-led interview with a standardized survey on migration policy among 571 respondents recruited via Prolific and Payback Panel. The findings establish AI Conversational Interviewing as a viable and valuable addition to the social-science toolkit. The conversational transcripts surface considerations and reasoning that a comprehensive standardized battery does not capture such as markedly different mental models of migration among subgroups with similar attitudes levels. Among respondents who completed the interview, evaluations of the AI interview were at or above those of the standardized survey across modes, although completion itself varied by condition. By releasing open data and open-source pipeline materials, the study contributes to a growing literature on harnessing artificial intelligence to expand the methods of public opinion measurement.

</details>


### [199] [Editorial Alignment: A Participatory Approach to Engaging Editorial Expertise in LLM-mediated Knowledge Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20258)
*Simon Aagaard Enni,Malthe Stavning Erslev,Karl-Emil Kjær Bilstrup,Kristoffer Laigaard Nielbo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何通过参与式设计工作坊，让公共知识机构的编辑参与重新对齐LLM接口，以符合编辑标准。研究以Nordic公共知识机构为案例，设计并实现了LLM驱动的百科全书界面，提出了"编辑对齐"（editorial alignment）作为参与式AI的设计实践，将编辑标准作为设计制品，将编辑实践和价值观转化为技术对齐目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的信息服务威胁着公共知识机构的编辑职能，预训练的LLM已经内嵌了商业开发者的价值观和传播策略，编辑权威受到挑战。需要探索如何让编辑参与LLM接口的对齐过程，以维护公共知识机构的编辑标准。

Method: 以Nordic公共知识机构为案例，通过设计工作坊（design workshops）让编辑参与LLM界面的重新对齐，设计并实现了一个LLM驱动的百科全书接口。

Result: 提出了"编辑对齐"作为参与式AI的设计实践，将AI对齐视为设计过程，将编辑标准定位为设计制品，使编辑实践和价值观可转化为技术实现的对齐目标。

Conclusion: 编辑对齐可以为编辑创造持续的参与空间，赋予编辑在LLM中介的知识传播中的能动性，维护公共知识机构的编辑权威。

Abstract: The emergence of LLM-driven information services is reshaping the conditions under which public knowledge institutions operate, threatening to absorb the editorial function these institutions exist to exercise. While LLMs offer powerful new affordances for knowledge dissemination, editorial authority is challenged by pretrained LLMs that arrive already aligned with the values and dissemination strategies of their commercial developers. This paper investigates editor participation in re-aligning LLM interfaces to editorial standards through design workshops, in a case study where we design and implement an LLM-enabled encyclopedia interface with a Nordic public knowledge institution. We introduce editorial alignment as a design practice within Participatory AI, framing AI alignment as a design process and positioning the editorial standard as a design artefact that translates editorial practice and values into alignment objectives for technical implementation. Last, we discuss how editorial alignment can create space for ongoing participation and give editors agency in LLM-mediated knowledge dissemination.

</details>


### [200] [Organizing in the Digital Age: Understanding Community, Challenges, and Consequences in Digitally-facilitated Labor Organizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20375)
*Frederick Reiber,Alishah Chator,Dana Calacci,Allison McDonald*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对17次定性访谈，深入分析了美国工会工作者如何利用Discord、WhatsApp和Slack等数字文本通信平台进行劳工组织，发现数字工具虽对当代劳工成功至关重要，但也带来了技术安全、信息过载和建立信任等新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代美国劳动力高度分散，工会组织需要借助数字通信工具来弥合时空差距，因此有必要系统研究这些工具在实际劳工组织中的应用情况、挑战与机遇。

Method: 采用17次定性访谈的研究方法，对多个工会中工作者使用Discord、WhatsApp和Slack等数字文本通信平台的情况进行深入分析。

Result: 数字工具是当代劳工成功不可或缺的组成部分，但同时也引入了技术安全、信息过载、信任与共识建立等新的复杂性挑战。

Conclusion: 研究结论将数字组织实践与更广泛的数字组织理论联系起来，揭示了数字工具在工会中扮演的双重角色——既是推动力也是挑战源。

Abstract: The contemporary American labor force is highly dispersed, necessitating the use of digital communication tools to bridge spatial and temporal gaps in union organizing. This study provides an in-depth analysis of how workers within various labor unions utilize digital, text-based communication platforms -- including Discord, WhatsApp, and Slack -- for labor organizing. Through 17 qualitative interviews, we examine the challenges and opportunities presented by digital organizing, identifying both technical and social obstacles. Our findings reveal that although digital tools are integral to contemporary labor successes, they also introduce new complexities, such as navigating technical security, managing information overload, and building trust and consensus. Based on these insights, we draw connections to broader understandings of digital organizing and the role of digital tools in unions.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Design Considerations for Phase Modulation in Testable Photonic Systems and Co-packaged Optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19674)
*Pratishtha Agnihotri,Priyank Kalla,Steve Blair*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文比较了硅光子集成电路中热诱导相位调制与基于载流子的电学调制在Mach-Zehnder和微环调制器中的性能，评估了消光比、调谐效率、功耗和调制带宽等关键指标，并总结了不同工作模式下两种方法的权衡与适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硅光子集成电路复杂度提升，测试和校准越来越依赖有效的相位调制机制，需要系统比较不同调制方法以指导实际设计选择。

Method: 设计并制作了热调制和电调制两种类型的Mach-Zehnder调制器和微环调制器，从消光比、调谐效率、功耗和调制带宽等维度进行对比评估。

Result: 明确了热调制与电学调制在调制速度、能耗和调谐可控性之间的关键权衡，揭示了两种方法在不同工作区间各自的优势与局限性。

Conclusion: 为在可扩展硅光子系统中集成测试与校准功能时选择相位调制策略提供了实用的设计指导。

Abstract: As silicon photonic integrated circuits (PICs) scale in complexity, testing and calibration increasingly depend on effective phase modulation mechanisms. This work compares thermally induced phase modulation and carrier-based electrical modulation in Mach-Zehnder and microring modulators. The devices are designed and evaluated for extinction ratio, tuning efficiency, power consumption, and modulation bandwidth. The study identifies key trade-offs among modulation speed, energy consumption, and tuning controllability that directly influence the suitability of these methods for test signal generation and calibration tasks. The results highlight the relative advantages and limitations of thermal and electrical approaches across different operating regimes. These findings provide practical design guidance for selecting phase modulation strategies in scalable silicon photonic systems with integrated test and calibration requirements.

</details>


### [202] [A Novel FeFET Differential Bit-Cell With Hybrid Volatile and Non-Volatile Memory Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19918)
*Jianze Wang,Wei Zhang,Xuanyao Fong*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由交叉耦合FeFET和两个存取晶体管组成的4T nvSRAM位单元，面积小于传统6T SRAM，可通过调整写入条件在易失性和非易失性模式间切换，非易失模式下存储功耗0.13μW，存储时间2ns，且无需显式备份与恢复操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决CMOS SRAM的高泄漏功耗和新兴非易失存储技术的大写入延迟问题，同时克服现有nvSRAM设计需要备份/恢复操作且面积开销大的缺陷。

Method: 提出一种差分存储位单元，采用一对交叉耦合FeFET和一对存取晶体管构成4T结构，通过调整写入条件配置为易失性或非易失性工作模式。

Result: 非易失模式下存储功耗0.13μW，存储时间2ns，无需显式备份与恢复操作；位单元面积小于传统6T SRAM及许多现有nvSRAM设计。

Conclusion: 该4T FeFET nvSRAM位单元在面积效率和操作简便性上优于传统方案，且交叉耦合增益单元结构具有进一步应用潜力。

Abstract: Non-volatile SRAM (nvSRAM) designs have been investigated to address the high leakage power of CMOS-based SRAM and the large write latency of emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) technologies. However, prior nvSRAM designs that combine SRAM with eNVM devices typically require backup and restore (B\&R) operations and incur significant cell-area overhead. Here, we propose a differential memory bit-cell consisting of a pair of cross-coupled ferroelectric field-effect transistors (FeFETs) and a pair of access transistors, resulting in a four-transistor (4T) structure, which is smaller than conventional 6T SRAM and many prior nvSRAM designs. The proposed bit-cell can be configured to operate in either volatile or non-volatile mode by adjusting the write conditions. In the non-volatile mode, the proposed nvSRAM achieves a store power of 0.13~$μ$W with a 2~ns store time, and no explicit B\&R operation is required. The proposed bit-cell can also be viewed as a cross-coupled gain cell, enabling further applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Weight Adaptation for Improving Parallel Performance of Adaptive Stochastic Natural Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19861)
*Yutaro Yamada,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出WA-ASNG算法，通过引入权重自适应机制，在概率模型进化算法中自适应更新权重参数，以最大化期望目标值的改进，在二值优化问题中优于PBIL和ASNG，且对强噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率模型进化算法（如ASNG）虽能自适应调整学习率实现高效优化，但当种群规模增大时，如何设置合适的权重参数仍不明确，限制了并行评估大规模任务时的优化性能。

Method: WA-ASNG通过从自然梯度的累积中估计更新方向信号，然后利用梯度上升法自适应更新权重参数以最大化该信号。学习率自适应保证期望目标值单调改进的充分条件，而权重自适应机制则旨在最大化这种改进。

Result: 实验结果表明，在二值优化问题中，种群规模从25到100的各种设置下，WA-ASNG均优于PBIL和ASNG，且在强噪声环境下仍能高效运行。

Conclusion: 权重自适应机制能够有效提升概率模型进化算法在大规模种群下的优化性能，为并行评估时间密集型任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms are promising for black-box optimization. Specifically, the adaptive stochastic natural gradient (ASNG) adaptively updates its learning rate, a typical hyperparameter in probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms, thereby realizing efficient and robust optimization. Although weight parameters are common hyperparameters, with the increasing demand for parallel evaluation of time-consuming tasks, it remains unclear how to set suitable weights for larger population sizes. In this paper, we propose Weight Adaptation ASNG (WA-ASNG), which incorporates a weight adaptation mechanism into ASNG. We calculated the estimated signal of the update direction from the accumulations of the natural gradient. Then, to maximize the signal, WA-ASNG adaptively updates its weight parameters by a gradient ascent over the optimization. While the learning rate adaptation plays a role in satisfying a sufficient condition for monotonic improvement of the expected objective value, the mechanism of weight adaptation is intended to maximize this improvement. The experimental results demonstrate that WA-ASNG outperforms PBIL and ASNG across various settings with population sizes ranging from 25 to 100 for binary optimization problems. Furthermore, WA-ASNG can perform efficiently in the presence of strong noise. Our code is available at https://github.com/shiralab/WA-ASNG .

</details>


### [204] [Hybrid ANN-SNN Pipeline with Local Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20151)
*Denis Larionov,Khairutin Shtanchaev,Mikhail Kiselev,Mikhail Korovin,Ivan Tugoy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合ANN-SNN流水线，将预训练人工神经网络（EfficientNet编码器）与脉冲神经网络分类器（CoLaNET）结合。通过速率编码将激活转化为脉冲序列，并利用局部生物启发学习规则训练SNN，在64类ImageNet任务上达到99.09%准确率，性能媲美传统深度网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用预训练ANN的丰富嵌入能力提升SNN性能，同时避免端到端梯度传播的计算开销，探索兼具生物合理性与高效率的混合框架。

Method: 采用预训练EfficientNet作为编码器，通过速率编码将其激活转换为脉冲序列，输入CoLaNET脉冲分类器；分类器使用局部生物启发学习规则（而非反向传播）进行训练，形成混合ANN-SNN流水线。

Result: 在64类ImageNet基准上达到99.09%准确率，性能可与传统深度网络相媲美。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种生物合理且高效的框架，可将强大的预训练编码器适配到下游SNN任务中，证明了混合架构在平衡性能与生物可解释性方面的潜力。

Abstract: This work proposes a hybrid ANN-SNN pipeline that effectively leverages the rich embeddings of pretrained artificial neural networks (ANNs) to enable high-performance spiking neural networks (SNNs). The architecture couples a pretrained EfficientNet encoder with a CoLaNET spiking classifier. We convert the encoder's activations into spike trains via rate-coding and train the subsequent SNN classifier using local, biologically inspired learning rules, bypassing end-to-end gradient propagation. This approach achieves 99.09% accuracy on a 64-class ImageNet benchmark, demonstrating performance on par with conventional deep networks. The work presents a biologically plausible and efficient framework for adapting powerful pretrained encoders to downstream spiking neural network tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Simulating Robotic Locomotion in Sand: Resistive Force Theory in an Open-Source Physics Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19504)
*Ryan Walker Brown,Laura K. Treers,Kathryn A. Daltorio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现了3D颗粒阻力理论（3D RFT），用于模拟机器人在沙地等颗粒介质上的行走。实验验证表明，该方法能够将六足机器人的行走距离和足部下陷深度控制在实验值的20%误差范围内，为机器人设计提供了高效的仿真工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFT方法能近似计算沙地行走的地面反作用力，但缺乏在3D物理引擎中的集成，无法用于机器人仿真。作者旨在填补这一空白，验证阻力近似方法是否足以提供稳定的自由行走仿真环境。

Method: 在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现3D颗粒阻力理论（3D RFT），通过多场景仿真验证末端执行器形状、速度、负载等关键趋势的一致性，并以12自由度六足机器人为对象进行实验比对。

Result: 仿真预测的六足机器人行走距离和足部下陷深度与实验值误差在20%以内。

Conclusion: 尽管RFT存在固有近似，该开源工具可用于开发和改进穿越颗粒介质的新型机器人设计。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Resistive Force Theory (RFT) enable approximation of ground reaction forces for locomotion in sand without the computational expense of modeling interactions with individual grains. However, these tools have been absent in 3D physics engines commonly used for robot simulation. We explore if resistive force approximations are sufficient, when integrated with standard dynamics calculations, to provide a stable substrate for a freely walking robot. To determine this, we implement 3D Granular Resistive Force Theory (3D RFT) in a physics simulation engine, MuJoCo. We verify simulations in multiple scenarios to demonstrate that key trends due to end effector shape, speed, and loading are preserved. Our implementation predicts walking distance and foot sinkage of a 12-Degree of Freedom hexapod robot within 20\% of experiments in sand. While RFT has inherent approximations, the open source tool described here has potential to help develop new and improved robot designs to traverse granular media substrates.

</details>


### [206] [Physical Atari: A Robust and Accessible Platform for Real-time Reinforcement Learning on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19357)
*Khurram Javed,Joseph Modayil,Gloria Kennickell,Richard S. Sutton,John Carmack*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为Physical Atari的物理强化学习研究平台，由Robotroller机器人（操作Atari CX40+控制器）和Atari Devbox（渲染游戏画面与奖励信号）组成，总成本低于1000美元，可进行数周不间断的强化学习实验。研究发现，学习与部署之间的微小分布偏移会显著降低策略性能，强调了设备端自适应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在物理世界中研究强化学习算法，需要构建一个鲁棒且易获取的实验平台，以弥合模拟环境与真实机器人之间的差距。

Method: 设计Robotroller（通过轴承减少磨损、高频伺服状态监控干预）和Atari Devbox（渲染ALE游戏画面与奖励信号），结合现成摄像头与台式电脑构建Physical Atari系统。

Result: Physical Atari可在低于1000美元成本下运行数周无机械故障的强化学习实验；验证了算法可直接在机器人上学习；发现学习与部署间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。

Conclusion: Physical Atari是一个鲁棒且经济的物理强化学习研究平台，实验结果强调了设备端自适应对于机器人在真实世界中获得强性能的关键作用。

Abstract: We built a robot called the Robotroller that actuates an Atari CX40+ controller and a device called the Atari Devbox that renders the game frame and the reward signal from the Arcade Learning Environment on a screen. The Robotroller and the Atari Devbox, together with an off-the-shelf camera and a desktop computer, constitute a system that can be used to study reinforcement learning algorithms in the physical world. We call the full system Physical Atari. In this paper, we detail the key decisions that make Physical Atari a robust and accessible platform. To make the system robust, we designed the Robotroller so that all movement is done through bearings, which reduces wear. Additionally, we wrote software that monitors the state of the servos at a high frequency and intervenes to limit stress. To make the system accessible, we used affordable off-the-shelf components and parts that can be manufactured using consumer 3D printers. Physical Atari can be built for under $1,000 and has been used for weeks of non-stop reinforcement learning experiments without any mechanical failures. We used it to validate that reinforcement learning algorithms can learn directly on robots and show that even small distribution shifts between learning and deployment can significantly degrade the performance of policies. Our results underscore the importance of on-device adaptation for strong performance on robots.

</details>


### [207] [Proprioceptive Invariant State Estimation for Humanoid Robots on Non-Inertial Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19512)
*Falak Mandali,Zijian He,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）的方法，利用足部IMU和运动学约束，仅通过本体感知实现人形机器人在非惯性地面上的实时状态估计，相比现有InEKF方法，收敛速度提升96%，位置估计误差降低80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人状态估计方法大多假设地面是静止或惯性的，无法处理非惯性地面（如摇摆、旋转的地面）下的估计问题。本文旨在利用纯本体感知传感器，在不依赖外部测量或地面运动先验知识的情况下，估计机器人相对于移动地面的位姿和速度。

Method: 采用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）框架，通过足部IMU利用支撑脚的运动学约束，将地面引起的非线性纳入过程和测量模型。滤波器设计为右不变测量模型，以获得有利的误差动态特性。通过可观测性分析建立了相对位姿和速度可观测的条件。

Result: 在Digit人形机器人上，站立和蹲踞于摇摆俯冲地面上的实验表明，相比现有InEKF，收敛速度提升96%，位置估计误差降低80%。在单轴旋转地面上的行走实验中，初始误差高达1米时，平均估计误差小于9厘米。

Conclusion: 提出的InEKF方法能够有效估计人形机器人在非惯性地面上的状态，具有快速收敛和高精度特性，适用于仅依赖本体感知的真实场景。

Abstract: This paper presents an invariant extended Kalman filtering (InEKF) approach for real-time state estimation of humanoid robots operating on non-inertial ground using only onboard proprioceptive sensing. The proposed approach estimates the robot's base position and velocity relative to the moving ground frame without requiring direct measurements of ground motion or externally mounted sensors. By exploiting kinematic constraints at the stance foot through foot-mounted IMUs, the filter accounts for ground-induced nonlinearities in the process and measurement models while remaining fully proprioceptive. The estimator is formulated to admit a right-invariant measurement model, enabling favorable error dynamics under large initial uncertainties. Observability analysis establishes conditions under which the robot's relative base position and velocity are observable with respect to the non-inertial ground frame. Experiments with the Digit humanoid robot standing and squatting atop a swaying and pitching ground showcase a 96% speedup in convergence rate and an 80% reduction in position estimate errors over existing InEKFs. Walking experiments on a uni-axially rotating ground achieve an average estimation error of less than 9 cm for an initial error of up to 1 m.

</details>


### [208] [WorkBenchMark: A LEGO-Based Assembly Benchmark with an Assembly-by-Disassembly Baseline for the Smart Manufacturing League](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19358)
*Wenbo Ma,Daniel Swoboda,Matteo Tschesche,Till Hofmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LEGO Duplo的机器人装配基准WorkBenchMark，包含400个任务和四个复杂度等级，并提供基于装配即拆卸的规划基线方案，在各项指标上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配任务需要将低层操控与任务级符号推理结合，现有端到端学习方法无法可靠解决这一问题，因此需要标准化的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建了基于LEGO Duplo的400个装配任务（分四个复杂度层级），提出开放词汇感知的"装配即拆卸"规划基线方案，并在模拟环境中进行验证。

Result: 提出的基于规划的基线方法在所有复杂度层级上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作（VLA）方法。

Conclusion: 该基准、模拟环境和基线实现将开源发布，以支持机器人装配社区的研究发展。

Abstract: We introduceWorkBenchMark, a LEGO Duplo-based robotic assembly benchmark motivated by the RoboCup Smart Manufacturing League. Robotic assembly couples low-level manipulation with task-level symbolic reasoning under physical constraints, a combination that current end-to-end learning methods do not yet solve reliably. The benchmark provides 400 tasks across four complexity tiers. We provide an open-vocabulary perception, Assembly-by-Disassembly baseline solution. Our planning-based pipeline outperforms a modern vision-language-action approach across all tiers. The benchmark, simulation environment, and baseline implementation will be released openly to support the broader robotic assembly community.

</details>


### [209] [pdSTL: Probabilistic Differentiable Signal Temporal Logic for Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19561)
*Bennett Dogbey,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了pdSTL（概率可微信号时序逻辑）框架，将概率语义与可微鲁棒性相结合，用于在不确定环境中具有概率安全保证的轨迹优化，在仿真和真实四旋翼飞行实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信号时序逻辑（STL）扩展存在两个主要缺陷：要么缺乏可微性，无法用于基于梯度的优化；要么忽略了信念空间中的不确定性，无法处理随机动力学和感知噪声。因此需要一种既能提供概率语义保证又支持可微优化的统一框架。

Method: pdSTL：1）采用区间值概率语义计算保守的满足边界，并通过STL语法树组合传播；2）将时序鲁棒性评估建模为递归的LSTM式展开，实现线性时间的可微监控，适用于端到端轨迹优化。

Result: 在仿真障碍物规避、换道机动以及真实Crazyflie四旋翼飞行器在气动干扰下的实验中，pdSTL实现了高效的优化并提供了形式化概率保证，在保持安全裕度方面显著优于确定性可微STL。

Conclusion: pdSTL通过概率可微语义统一了安全保证与梯度优化的兼容性，有效应对了真实世界中的不确定性，为自主机器人在复杂环境中的安全轨迹优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in uncertain environments must satisfy complex temporal and safety specifications despite stochastic dynamics and sensing noise. While Signal Temporal Logic (STL) offers robustness measures for gradient-based optimization, existing extensions either lack differentiability or ignore belief-space uncertainty. We introduce pdSTL (probabilistic differentiable Signal Temporal Logic), a framework that unifies probabilistic semantics with differentiable robustness over belief trajectories. pdSTL employs interval-valued probabilistic semantics to compute conservative satisfaction bounds, propagated compositionally through the STL syntax tree. We formulate the temporal robustness evaluation as a recurrent, LSTM-style unfolding of STL operators, enabling linear-time, differentiable monitoring suitable for end-to-end trajectory optimization. We validate pdSTL on simulated obstacle avoidance, lane-change maneuvers, and real-world Crazyflie quadcopter flight experiments under aerodynamic disturbances. Results demonstrate that pdSTL achieves efficient optimization with formal probabilistic guarantees, significantly outperforming deterministic differentiable STL in maintaining safety margins under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [210] [3D Scene Graphs: Open Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19383)
*Dennis Rotondi,Francesco Argenziano,Sebastian Koch,Nathan Hughes,Martin Buechner,Johanna Wald,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Daniele Nardi,Abhinav Valada,Liam Paull,Federico Tombari,Luca Carlone,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对3D场景图（3DSG）进行了统一而批判性的综述，涵盖了其形式化定义、建模选择、构建流程、下游应用和评估策略，并重点指出了该领域存在的碎片化问题、开放挑战及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景图作为结合几何、语义和关系抽象的强大空间AI表示，已被广泛应用于机器人和计算机视觉的多个领域。然而，该领域存在碎片化问题：不同社区使用不同的形式化定义、构建流程和评估协议，导致方法难以比较、共同假设难以识别、实际部署中的挑战难以评估。为此，本文旨在提供一个统一的批判性综述，厘清现状并指出未来方向。

Method: 作者首先将3DSG置于一个通用定义下进行形式化，分析现有方法在节点和边属性、层次结构、动态场景表示及可操作性扩展等建模选择上的差异；接着回顾从原始传感器观测构建3DSG的常见术语、约定和技术；最后考察下游应用和评估策略，包括从内在图质量到任务级性能的多层次评估。

Result: 本文系统性地梳理了3DSG领域的核心建模选择与构建技术，揭示了不同社区间的形式化差异与共性，并识别出当前在鲁棒实际部署中的关键挑战。此外，作者提供了配套网站以组织与扩展综述内容。

Conclusion: 3DSG是一种富有表达力的空间AI表示，但领域碎片化严重；需要统一的形式化框架和评估标准以推动方法的可比性与实际部署。未来方向包括动态场景表示、可操作性扩展、鲁棒构建以及标准化评估协议等。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a powerful representation for spatial AI by combining geometric grounding with semantic and relational abstractions of the environment. Their expressiveness has made them relevant to a broad range of problems in robotics and computer vision, including manipulation, navigation, task planning, scene understanding, and many others. However, the field remains fragmented: different communities adopt distinct formulations, construction pipelines, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to compare methods, identify common assumptions, and assess remaining challenges for robust real-world deployment. This survey provides a unified and critical review of 3DSGs, with particular emphasis on open challenges and future directions. We first formalize 3DSGs under a common definition and analyze the principal modeling choices that characterize existing formulations, including node and edge attributes, hierarchical structure, dynamic scene representations, and affordance-aware extensions. We then review how 3DSGs are built from raw sensory observations, discussing the most common terminologies, conventions, and techniques. Finally, we examine downstream applications and evaluation strategies, from intrinsic graph quality to task-level performance. To support the community, we also provide a dedicated website that organizes and extends the surveyed content, accessible at https://3dscenegraphs.com/.

</details>


### [211] [Safe, Real-Time Active Model Discrimination and Fault Diagnosis for Nonlinear Systems via Differentiable Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19590)
*Xinpei Ni,Melkior Ornik,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于不确定连续时间非线性系统的安全、实时的主动故障诊断与模型判别算法。通过区间过近似可达集和可微诊断目标，在数十毫秒内实现可靠模型判别，同时提供形式化安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在过程和测量扰动的非线性系统中，现有主动故障诊断方法难以同时满足实时性、安全约束和确定性的模型判别，尤其在多故障模式和高维机器人系统中。

Method: 提出输出反馈时变策略优化问题，利用区间过近似对可达状态和输出集进行可靠近似，通过可微目标函数惩罚候选模型可达输出集之间的重叠来编码可诊断性，并使用JAX和可微可达性原语实现高效的基于梯度的在线优化。

Result: 在多个高维非线性机器人系统（包括四旋翼、战斗机模型、差分驱动机器人和四足导航）上，在不到50毫秒内实现可靠的模型判别，在判别成功率和速度上均优于基线方法，并提供了形式化安全保证。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在保证安全约束的前提下，实现高效的实时主动故障诊断，适用于多种高维非线性机器人系统。

Abstract: We present a safe, real-time algorithm for active fault diagnosis and model discrimination for uncertain continuous-time nonlinear systems with process and measurement disturbances. Given a finite set of candidate models representing nominal and faulty modes, including actuator and sensor faults, we formulate an output-feedback, time-varying policy optimization problem that (i) robustly enforces state-input safety constraints over a finite horizon and (ii) drives the system to produce sampled measurements consistent with at most one model, enabling deterministic diagnosis. To solve this problem in real time, we develop a tractable approximation using interval over-approximations of reachable state and output sets, and encode diagnosability via a differentiable objective that penalizes overlap between the reachable output sets of possible models. The resulting optimization is solved efficiently online with gradient-based methods using JAX and differentiable reachability primitives. We evaluate our method on sensor and actuator fault diagnosis (up to 11 fault modes) in several high-dimensional nonlinear robotic systems, including a simulated quadrotor and fighter-jet model, a hardware differential-drive robot, and quadrupedal navigation. Across these case studies, our approach achieves reliable model discrimination in under 50 ms, outperforming baselines in discrimination success rate and speed while providing formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [212] [DiffusionVS: A Generative Framework for Robust Visual Servoing Based on Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19397)
*Hongkang Cui,Rui He,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散策略的视觉伺服方法，通过在线训练范式增强模型的泛化能力，在仿真和实际实验中分别达到近100%和93%的成功率，并验证了扩散机制可通用地提升现有视觉伺服网络的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于回归的视觉伺服方法存在两大问题：单步映射对噪声敏感导致轨迹抖动，以及分布偏移时误差累积。为此，本文引入扩散策略来提升时间一致性和鲁棒性。

Method: 基于Diffusion Policy，以标签角点的归一化图像坐标为输入，通过条件去噪生成相机速度；针对静态数据集训练的泛化局限，采用在线训练范式，通过交互式经验收集持续扩充训练数据多样性。

Result: 仿真成功率达近100%，实际实验成功率达93%。将扩散模块集成到现有视觉伺服网络中，均能一致提升其性能。

Conclusion: 所提策略具有广泛的适用性，可超越本文具体架构，增强各类视觉伺服系统。

Abstract: Visual servoing is a fundamental technique in robotic manipulation and navigation. Regression-based visual servoing frequently experiences trajectory jitter as a result of noise-sensitive single-step mappings and the accumulation of errors during distribution shifts. In contrast, Diffusion Policy maintains temporal consistency by predicting action sequences and improves robustness through implicit data augmentation.
  This paper presents a novel diffusion-based servoing method. Based on Diffusion Policy, the proposed approach uses normalized image coordinates of observed tag corners as input and generates camera velocity through conditional denoising. To overcome the generalization limitations of models trained on static datasets, an online training paradigm is adopted, continuously expanding the diversity of training data through interactive experience collection. This strategy substantially enhances both the performance and generalization capability of the model. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, achieving success rates of nearly 100\% in simulation and 93\% in physical experiments. Beyond the specific pipeline, we further validate the generality of the diffusion mechanism. Experiments show that existing visual servoing networks consistently achieve improved performance when integrated with our diffusion-based module. These results indicate that the proposed strategy possesses broad applicability and can enhance various visual servoing systems beyond the specific architecture presented here.

</details>


### [213] [Comparative Study on Agility, Efficiency, and Impact Absorption of Bipedal Robots with Active Toes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19699)
*Joong-Gil Kim,Wontae Ye,Geunwoo Cho,Seong-Ho Yun,Se-Hyoung Cho,Yong-Jae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种14自由度仿人双足机器人，其脚趾设计模仿人类脚趾的轻量化、高扭矩和鲁棒性。通过高保真仿真训练环境，对比有无主动脚趾的配置，发现脚趾在1.33m/s行走时使运输成本降低17.5%，脚跟冲击力降低5.0%，路径偏差平均减少25.0%、最大减少34.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类腿部具有高效率、敏捷性和冲击吸收能力，脚趾在其中发挥关键作用。现有机器人虽尝试实现类人脚趾，但未能完全复现人类特征，也未严谨验证脚趾的实际益处。

Method: 设计了14自由度的双足机器人，模拟人类脚趾的轻量、高扭矩和鲁棒特性；构建了高保真仿真训练环境，反映实际执行器、耦合传动和精确功耗；采用最小化强化学习奖励函数，对有无主动脚趾的配置进行相同训练流程的公平对比。

Result: 在1.33m/s行走速度下，有脚趾配置相比无脚趾配置：运输成本降低17.5%，脚跟冲击力降低5.0%；敏捷性测试中，平均路径偏差减少25.0%，最大路径偏差减少34.0%。

Conclusion: 仿生主动脚趾能显著提升双足机器人的行走效率、冲击吸收能力和敏捷性，验证了脚趾在仿人机器人中的关键作用。

Abstract: Human legs exhibit high efficiency, agility, and impact absorption, with toes playing a crucial role in these capabilities. While many attempts have been made to implement human-like toes in robots, they have not fully replicated human characteristics nor rigorously validated their benefits. We propose a 14-DOF biped robot emulating human toes' lightweight, high-torque, robust nature. To quantitatively analyze the effectiveness of the active toes in terms of agility, efficiency, and impact absorption, we developed a high-fidelity simulation training environment that reflects actual actuators with coupled transmissions and accurate power consumption. To ensure a fair comparison between configurations with and without active toes, we designed a minimal RL reward function and applied an identical training procedure to both. The simulation results indicate that, at 1.33 m/s walking, the toe-equipped robot reduced CoT by 17.5% and heel-strike GRF by 5.0% compared with the toe-ablation configuration. On the agility test, average and maximum path deviation decreased by 25.0% and 34.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [214] [Playful Agentic Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19419)
*Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Hanjun Yoo,Letian Fu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Raj Saravanan,Shaofeng Yin,Justin Yu,Dantong Niu,Zirui Wang,Roei Herzig,Ken Goldberg,Yutong Bai,David M. Chan,Ion Stoica,Angjoo Kanazawa,Jiahui Lei,Haiwen Feng,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RATs（机器人智能体团队），一种通过自指导游戏（self-directed play）让机器人持续学习可复用代码技能的方法，在无明确指令下自主探索、执行、纠错并将成功经验蒸馏为技能库，显著提升下游任务表现，且技能库可零微调迁移至其他代码即策略智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体系统虽能编写可执行代码策略并根据反馈迭代修正，但本质上仍是任务驱动的——可复用技能仅在收到明确指令后才被习得。本文旨在让机器人在下游任务到来前，通过自指导游戏阶段持续学习技能，实现无需人工干预的自主技能积累。

Method: 提出RATs（机器人智能体团队），在游戏阶段自主提出新颖但可学习的探索性任务，规划并执行机器人代码策略，验证中间进展，诊断失败原因，通过密集步骤级反馈进行重试，并将成功执行蒸馏为持久化的代码技能库。测试时，从冻结的技能库中检索相关技能辅助解决新任务。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces上，游戏习得技能相比无游戏和随机游戏基线分别提升20.6和17.0个百分点。技能库可零微调迁移至其他测试时代码即策略智能体，在RoboSuite和真实世界迁移中分别提升8.9和8.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 自指导游戏阶段学习的技能能显著提升下游任务表现，且技能库具有良好的可迁移性和泛化能力，无需对底层模型进行微调即可提升多种代码即策略智能体的性能。

Abstract: Current agentic robot systems can write executable Code-as-Policy programs, observe feedback, and revise behavior across multiple attempts, but they remain largely task-driven: reusable skills are acquired only after explicit instructions. We study Playful Agentic Robot Learning, where an embodied coding agent uses self-directed play as a continual skill-learning stage before downstream tasks arrive. We introduce RATs, Robotics Agent Teams designed for play-time skill acquisition. During play, RATs proposes novel yet learnable exploratory tasks, plans and executes robot-code policies, verifies intermediate progress, diagnoses failures, retries with dense, step-level feedback, and distills successful executions into a persistent code skill library. At test time, the agent reuses relevant skills from this frozen library to help solve new tasks. Experiments in LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces show that play-learned skills improve held-out downstream tasks over no-play and random-play baselines, with 20.6 and 17.0 percentage-point gains over CaP-Agent0 on LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces, respectively. Moreover, the learned skills can be plugged into other inference-time Code-as-Policy agents by simply retrieving them into the context, improving RoboSuite and real-world transfer by 8.9 and 8.8 points, respectively, without finetuning the underlying model.

</details>


### [215] [A Differentiable Composite Approximation Framework for Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Maneuvering Modeling from Sea-Trial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19711)
*Aobo Wang,Aifei Xia,Zihao Wang,Lizhu Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可微复合逼近方法，将多项式基与数据自适应基（神经网络）联合校准，结合转向运动海流估计补偿，用于全尺寸AUV操纵建模。海试数据验证表明，该方法在递归轨迹与速度预测上优于纯多项式、纯神经网络及冻结先验混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操纵建模方法中，传统方法使用预定义多项式基（受限于固定形式），数据驱动方法使用数据自适应基。从基函数视角出发，本文旨在将两类基函数作为统一预测器的可微部分进行联合校准，以融合两者优势。

Method: 提出可微复合逼近公式：多项式基分量与数据自适应基（神经网络残差）作为单一预测器的可微部分；开发基于梯度的联合校准方法，含敏感度感知机制约束多项式更新；引入转向运动海流估计补偿流程，构建海流补偿学习目标。

Result: 在7米AUV多操纵条件下的海试数据上，所提方法在递归轨迹与速度预测方面优于纯多项式、纯神经网络及冻结先验混合基线方法。

Conclusion: 该可微复合逼近框架有效融合了物理先验（多项式基）与数据驱动自适应能力（神经网络），结合海流补偿，适用于基于海试数据的全尺寸AUV操纵建模。

Abstract: Field-based modeling from onboard measurements can produce autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) maneuvering models that reflect real operating characteristics. From an approximation perspective, conventional maneuvering models use predefined constraint polynomial bases, whereas data-driven models use data-adaptive bases. Motivated by this basis-function view, this paper presents a differentiable composite-approximation formulation, in which the polynomial-basis component and the data-adaptive basis component are treated as differentiable parts of a single predictor and calibrated jointly. A gradient-based co-calibration method is developed for full-scale AUV maneuvering prediction, where a sensitivity-aware mechanism regulates bounded polynomial updates while the neural residual captures remaining nonlinear discrepancies under a shared prediction objective. To account for ocean-current effects in field data, a turning-motion-based current estimation and compensation procedure is incorporated to construct current-compensated learning targets for training and rollout. The framework is evaluated using sea-trial data collected from a 7-meter AUV under multiple maneuvering conditions. Results show that the proposed method improves recursive trajectory and velocity prediction compared with polynomial-only, neural-only, and frozen-prior hybrid baselines, demonstrating its applicability to field-data-based AUV maneuvering modeling.

</details>


### [216] [A Categorial and Sheaf-Theoretic Semantics for Autonomic Component Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19525)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用层论（sheaf theory）对SCEL形式化模型进行重建模，将分布式自主智能体系统的验证问题转化为拓扑空间的几何分析，通过层上同调量化系统故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自主智能体系统（如机器人集群、网络物理系统）对传统形式化方法构成挑战，SCEL的操作语义不适于推理全局性、结构性和涌现性属性。

Method: 利用范畴论和层论构建SCEL的多层数学模型：将组件视为拓扑空间中的点，组件集合视为开集，分布式知识视为层的数据；信息共享等价于层的"粘合"操作，系统故障视为拓扑障碍并由层上同调量化。

Result: 该框架将复杂分布式系统的验证转化为数学对象的几何分析，为鲁棒自主系统的设计提供结构性洞察。

Conclusion: 层论方法能够从拓扑与几何角度深刻理解分布式系统的全局属性，有望成为自主智慧系统验证与设计的有力工具。

Abstract: The proliferation of large-scale, decentralized systems of autonomous agents, such as swarms of robots and networked cyber-physical systems, presents a formidable challenge to traditional formal methods. The Software Component Ensemble Language (SCEL) offers a formal model for such systems, but its operational semantics is not ideal for reasoning about global, structural, and emergent properties. This report proposes a new, multi-layered mathematical model for SCEL using category theory and sheaf theory. We argue that a society of robots described in SCEL can be formally modeled as a sheaf on a topological space, where components are points, ensembles are open sets, and distributed knowledge forms the sheaf's data. In this framework, computational processes like information sharing become equivalent to the sheaf-theoretic operation of "gluing" local data. System failures can then be understood and quantified as topological obstructions, measurable by sheaf cohomology. This approach transforms the verification of a complex distributed system into the analysis of the geometry of a mathematical object, providing deep, structural insights for the design of robust autonomic systems.

</details>


### [217] [SCAN-Planner: Spatial Collision-Aware Local Planning for Route-Guided Long-Range Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19555)
*Han Zheng,Zhe Chen,Yiwen Fu,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAN-Planner提出了一种空间碰撞感知的局部规划框架，用于四足机器人在狭窄通道、杂乱室内和大型3D非结构化环境中的长距离导航。该方法采用偏航感知的双圆柱足迹建模机器人细长身体，通过稀疏查询膨胀3D占用地图实现全身碰撞评估，并引入投影A*搜索在插值的地面跟随曲面上生成无碰撞引导，同时通过z梯度抑制实现水平避障和垂直稳定性。仿真和真实实验验证了其在密集杂乱、3D非结构化场景、楼梯 traversal 和长距离导航中生成安全、平滑、高效轨迹的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部规划器通常使用各向同性的几何膨胀或依赖平面/高程图表示来近似机器人，导致在狭窄空间中的运动过于保守，且对悬垂结构的推理能力有限。

Method: 1) 偏航感知的双圆柱足迹模型；2) 在膨胀3D占用图中通过稀疏查询进行全身碰撞评估；3) 投影A*搜索在插值地面跟随曲面上生成无碰撞引导，配合z梯度抑制实现水平避障与垂直稳定性；4) 机器人中心滑动地图与边界回退机制支持大规模部署和局部死胡同恢复。

Result: 在密集杂乱、3D非结构化场景、楼梯 traversal 和长距离导航任务中，SCAN-Planner生成了安全、平滑且高效的轨迹。

Conclusion: 所提出的空间碰撞感知规划框架有效解决了四足机器人在复杂环境中的全身碰撞推理问题，优于依赖各向同性膨胀或平面/高程图表示的保守方法。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly expected to navigate through narrow passages, cluttered indoor scenes, and large-scale 3D unstructured environments. Existing local planners commonly approximate the robot using isotropic geometric inflation or rely on planar and elevation-map representations, leading to conservative motion in tight spaces and limited reasoning about overhanging structures. This letter presents SCAN-Planner, a spatial collision-aware local planning framework for long-range quadruped navigation. A yaw-aware twin-cylinder footprint is used to model the elongated robot body, enabling whole-body collision evaluation through sparse queries in an inflated 3D occupancy map. We further introduce a projected A* search that generates collision-free guidance on an interpolated ground-following surface, with z-gradient suppression to avoid obstacles horizontally while maintaining vertical stability. For large-scale deployment, a robot-centric sliding map with boundary fallback provides high-resolution local collision checking and recovery from local dead ends. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that SCAN-Planner generates safe, smooth, and efficient trajectories in dense clutter, 3D unstructured scenes, stair traversal, and long-range navigation tasks.

</details>


### [218] [One Demo is Worth a Thousand Trajectories: Action-View Augmentation for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19586)
*Chuer Pan,Litian Liang,Dominik Bauer,Eric Cousineau,Benjamin Burchfiel,Siyuan Feng,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯泼溅和轨迹优化的数据增强框架，通过生成视觉真实的鱼眼图像序列和物理可行的动作轨迹，提升机械臂操作策略在分布外场景（如障碍物、初始配置变化）下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略对机器人初始配置和未见障碍物敏感，易导致分布外观测和执行失败，而大规模数据收集成本高昂，亟需高效的数据增强方法。

Method: 1) 提出适配广角鱼眼相机的高斯泼溅公式，重建和编辑含未见物体的3D场景；2) 利用轨迹优化生成平滑无碰撞且利于视图渲染的动作轨迹；3) 从新颖视角渲染视觉观测，生成视觉真实的鱼眼图像序列及对应物理可行的动作轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该增强框架在相同场景和含障碍物的增强场景中均显著提升了多种操作任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 该数据增强框架有效提升了操作策略在分布外场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为减少数据收集成本提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for manipulation have demonstrated remarkable potential in modeling complex robotic behaviors, yet minor alterations in the robot's initial configuration and unseen obstacles easily lead to out-of-distribution observations. Without extensive data collection effort, these result in catastrophic execution failures. In this work, we introduce an effective data augmentation framework that generates visually realistic fisheye image sequences and corresponding physically feasible action trajectories from real-world eye-in-hand demonstrations, captured with a portable parallel gripper with a single fisheye camera. We introduce a novel Gaussian Splatting formulation, adapted to wide FoV fisheye cameras, to reconstruct and edit the 3D scene with unseen objects. We utilize trajectory optimization to generate smooth, collision-free, view-rendering-friendly action trajectories and render visual observations from corresponding novel views. Comprehensive experiments in simulation and the real world show that our augmentation framework improves the success rate for various manipulation tasks in both the same scene and the augmented scene with obstacles requiring collision avoidance.

</details>


### [219] [Fail-RAG : A Retrieval Augmented Generation Informed Framework for Robot Failure Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19598)
*Ameya Salvi,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Fail-RAG，一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的机器人操作故障检测框架，通过将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入并与故障数据库进行相似度检索，再结合视觉语言模型（VLM）进行详细分析。在仓库自动化场景中，针对五种机器人操作类型，相比直接使用VLM，Fail-RAG的故障检测准确率平均提升25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库环境中机器人执行物料搬运任务时，可能遭遇各种意外事件（故障），而基于规则的检测方法难以应对环境和任务动态变化导致的故障形态改变，因此需要更鲁棒的故障检测方法。

Method: 提出Fail-RAG框架：将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入向量，与故障数据库进行相似度检索；随后使用视觉语言模型（VLM）按照指令模板对检索到的故障进行详细分析，实现故障检测。

Result: 在固定机械臂和移动操作机器人上，针对五种仓库自动化常见任务进行仿真和物理实验。Fail-RAG相比直接使用现成VLM，故障检测准确率平均提升25个百分点。

Conclusion: Fail-RAG结合检索增强生成与视觉语言模型，在动态仓库环境中能有效检测机器人操作故障，优于直接使用VLM的基线方法，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Industry automation is witnessing an evolution in robotics driven by both technological breakthroughs and societal changes: progress towards generalist robots, embodied and physical artificial intelligence (AI), and increasing labor shortage in manufacturing.An intelligent autonomous robot needs to not only act according to planned motions but also react to any unexpected events. In this study, we focus on such unexpected events in warehouses where robots are used for material handling. Specifically, we refer to any unexpected events as failures and develop methods to detect robot operations related failures. Rule-based detection methods may break since the form of failures could change due to the dynamic nature of both environments and tasks. We propose 'Fail-RAG', a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG)-based failure detection framework where failure images and context information are embedded and queried against a failure database by calculating their similarities. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are further used to analyze failures and provide details by following our instruction template. We evaluated the performance of Fail-RAG by conducting both simulation and physical experiments using fixed robot arms and a mobile manipulator for multiple tasks that are common in warehouse automation. Fail-RAG achieved 25 percentage point higher failure detection accuracy on average across five types of robot operations compared to using off-the-shelf VLMs, indicating its effectiveness for real-world failure detection.

</details>


### [220] [Formal Verification of Learned Multi-Agent Communication Policies via Decision Tree Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19632)
*Ahmad Farooq,Kamran Iqbal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向多智能体强化学习通信策略的形式化安全验证框架，通过策略抽象（决策树蒸馏）将神经策略转化为可解释模型，结合PRISM模型检测器验证PCTL时态逻辑属性，在5-7智能体无人机编队中实现88.9%属性满足率，所有安全阈值通过验证，并证明验证属性可迁移回原始网络（偏差≤0.6个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在无人机群和自动驾驶车队等安全关键机器人部署中缺乏形式化安全保障，现有神经策略无法提供严格的安全性保证。

Method: 四阶段流水线：(1) 领域特定特征提取；(2) 决策树蒸馏（97.9%保真度）；(3) 自动翻译为PRISM概率模型检测器规约；(4) 基于两两分解与并集边界聚合的组合式PCTL属性验证，结合经验邻居建模。

Result: 在5-7智能体多无人机协调任务中验证18个时态逻辑属性，88.9%满足率，所有5个安全阈值通过（碰撞概率0.3% vs阈值1%）；蒙特卡洛验证显示偏差≤0.6个百分点；离散VQ-VIB消息相比连续方法保真度提升+11.6至+13.6个百分点，验证速度提升3-4倍。

Conclusion: 所提框架为深度MARL与形式化安全工作流之间提供了实用桥梁，通过经验验证的策略抽象实现了可迁移的形式化安全验证，适合多机器人部署场景。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables agents to develop coordination strategies through emergent communication, but neural policies lack the formal safety guarantees required for safety-critical robotic deployment in drone swarms and autonomous vehicle fleets. We present the first end-to-end framework for safety verification of learned multi-agent communication policies through policy abstraction: neural policies are distilled into interpretable decision trees, then formally verified, with empirical validation confirming that verified safety properties transfer to original networks. Our four-stage pipeline consists of domain-specific feature extraction from agent observations, decision tree distillation achieving 97.9% +/- 1.2% fidelity to neural policies, automated translation to PRISM probabilistic model checker specifications with complete feature-to-state-variable correspondence, and compositional verification of Probabilistic Computation Tree Logic (PCTL) properties via pairwise decomposition with union-bound aggregation and empirical neighbor modeling. Evaluating Vector-Quantized Variational Information Bottleneck (VQ-VIB) policies for multi-drone coordination with 5-7 agents, we verify 18 temporal logic properties across safety, liveness, and cooperation, achieving 88.9% property satisfaction with all five safety thresholds satisfied (0.3% collision probability vs. 1% threshold). Monte Carlo validation of original neural policies confirms that verified safety properties transfer with <=0.6 percentage-point deviation (95% CI). Discrete VQ-VIB messages provide +11.6 to +13.6 percentage-point fidelity advantages over continuous methods, enabling 3-4x faster verification. Our framework provides empirically validated safety verification for distilled policy abstractions, serving as a practical bridge between deep MARL and formal safety workflows for multi-robot deployment.

</details>


### [221] [CTS-MoE: Implicit Terrain Adaptation via Mixture-of-Experts for Perceptive Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19633)
*Francisco Affonso,Matheus P. Angarola,Ana Luiza Mineiro,Aditya Potnis,Marcelo Becker,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CTS-MoE框架，通过密集混合专家actor与基于感知的门控机制结合多critic任务特定价值头，实现四足机器人在非连续地形上的自适应运动，在模拟和实际实验中优于单一体基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非连续地形（楼梯、间隙、障碍物）上的感知四足运动需要适应性行为，单一保守步态无法应对突变的拓扑变化。多任务强化学习中存在共享与分离的矛盾——任务共享运动基础但奖励冲突，需要策略在共享行为的同时避免价值干扰。现有方法要么使用单一体策略牺牲特化能力，要么使用分层子策略牺牲跨转换和未见地形的泛化能力。

Method: 提出CTS-MoE，包含：(1) 密集混合专家actor（dense mixture-of-experts actor）结合基于感知的门控机制（perception-based gating）来组合共享行为；(2) 多critic结构（multi-critic）包含任务特定价值头（task-specific value heads）防止干扰；(3) 端到端单阶段并发教师-学生训练框架（single-stage concurrent teacher-student setup），处理部分可观测性，避免序列蒸馏，任务标签仅在训练时使用。部署时路由完全依赖感知，无需高层选择器或地形分类器。

Result: 在Unitree Go1上的仿真和硬件实验中，在已见和未见地形上均表现出任务感知的特化能力，相比单一体基线方法具有更低的跟踪误差和更高的成功率。

Conclusion: CTS-MoE通过密集MoE与多critic的有机结合，有效解决了多任务强化学习中共享与分离的张力，实现了无需高层选择器的感知驱动地形自适应运动，在非连续地形上取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion over discontinuous terrain (e.g., stairs, gaps, and obstacles) requires adaptive behavior, as a single conservative gait cannot produce the anticipatory maneuvers needed for abrupt topology changes. Cast as multi-task reinforcement learning, this problem introduces a tension between sharing and separation. Tasks use a common locomotion base but have conflicting rewards, so a policy must share behavior while avoiding value interference. Prior work addresses only one side, with monolithic policies sacrificing specialization and hierarchical sub-policies sacrificing generalization across transitions and unseen terrain. We propose CTS-MoE, which combines a dense mixture-of-experts actor with perception-based gating to compose shared behaviors and a multi-critic with task-specific value heads to prevent interference. The model is trained end-to-end in a single-stage concurrent teacher-student setup that handles partial observability and avoids sequential distillation, with task labels used only during training. At deployment, routing depends solely on perception, allowing terrain adaptation without a high-level selector or terrain classifier. Experiments on a Unitree Go1 in simulation and on hardware across seen and unseen terrains show task-aware specialization, with lower tracking error and higher success rates than monolithic baselines. Project Website: https://cts-moe.github.io/ .

</details>


### [222] [Scaling Self-Play for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19641)
*Luke Rowe,Roger Girgis,Rodrigue de Schaetzen,Daphne Cornelisse,Alaap Grandhi,Felix Heide,Eugene Vinitsky,Christopher Pal,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Gigapixel，一个高吞吐量的批处理驾驶模拟器，通过自对抗DAgger训练和特权教师蒸馏，实现基于像素观测的端到端驾驶模型的大规模自对抗训练，并在真实世界传感器数据上通过轻量级感知适应取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型通常使用离线人类演示数据集训练，存在状态覆盖有限且缺乏闭环反馈的问题，导致部署时出现复合误差且对长尾交互场景脆弱。现有自对抗方法虽然有效，但通常假设向量化鸟瞰图（BEV）观测，与直接基于传感器观测的端到端策略不兼容。

Method: 1) 构建Gigapixel：高吞吐量批处理驾驶模拟器，采用简化的边界框世界渲染（而非计算昂贵的光照真实感模拟），透视渲染，吞吐量达50k agent steps/s。2) 提出自对抗DAgger训练：通过在线策略蒸馏从特权RL教师模型训练基于像素的策略。3) 通过轻量级感知适应将自对抗训练的策略迁移到真实世界传感器数据。

Result: 在HUGSIM和NAVSIM-v2基准测试中，无需人类轨迹监督即可取得竞争性能。且扩展自对抗训练规模能带来策略性能的比例提升。

Conclusion: 自对抗训练是一种实用且可扩展的端到端驾驶模型训练策略，通过简化模拟器设计结合特权蒸馏与感知适应，有效弥合了模拟到现实的差距。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models are typically trained on offline human-demonstration datasets that provide limited state coverage and often no closed-loop feedback, making them prone to compounding errors when deployed in closed-loop and brittle to long-tail agent interactions. To overcome these limitations, we propose an alternative strategy for training end-to-end driving models: large-scale self-play directly from pixels in simulation. While prior self-play approaches have shown promising transfer to real-world driving, they typically assume vectorized Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) observations that are incompatible with end-to-end policies operating directly on sensor observations. To this end, we introduce Gigapixel, a high-throughput batched driving simulator with perspective rendering, enabling scalable self-play directly from pixel observations. Rather than targeting compute-costly photorealistic sensor simulation, Gigapixel renders a simplified bounding-box world that preserves essential scene structure while achieving throughput at 50k agent steps per second. Since direct pixel-space self-play RL is prohibitively sample-inefficient at end-to-end model scale, we propose self-play DAgger training: we train pixel-based policies in self-play via on-policy distillation from a privileged RL teacher. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we subsequently transfer the self-play trained policies to real-world sensor data through lightweight perception adaptation. Policies trained in Gigapixel and adapted to real-world sensor data achieve competitive performance on the HUGSIM and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks without human trajectory supervision. Moreover, scaling self-play training yields proportional gains in policy performance, establishing self-play as a practical and scalable strategy for training end-to-end models.

</details>


### [223] [DF-ExpEnse: Diffusion Filtered Exploration for Sample Efficient Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19656)
*Calvin Luo,Chen Sun,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DF-ExpEnse是一种用于预训练生成控制策略微调阶段的探索技术，通过构建可评估的候选动作集合并利用集成评论家选择最优动作，在单智能体和多智能体场景下均能提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将预训练的生成控制策略（已总结离线经验）适配到自收集的在线经验时，在线经验的质量直接影响微调效率。现有方法缺乏有效的探索机制来提升在线数据收集质量。

Method: 利用生成控制策略的多模态建模能力构建表达力强且可计算评估的候选动作集，再通过集成评论家（ensemble of critics）从中选择既能保持高质量又能最大化探索兴趣的动作。在多智能体场景中，进一步支持跨智能体通信以实现协作探索。

Result: 在多项操作和 locomotion 任务上，DF-ExpEnse 相较于默认微调方案和替代动作选择方案，均取得了一致的样本效率提升。

Conclusion: DF-ExpEnse可无缝集成现有的通过强化学习微调预训练生成控制策略的方法，有效提升在线经验收集质量，进而提高微调样本效率。

Abstract: A natural recipe for intelligent robotic decision-making is initializing from pretrained generative control policies, which have summarized offline experience, and adapting them to self-collected online experience. We present DF-ExpEnse, an exploration technique that improves the quality of online experience collection, thus increasing finetuning sample-efficiency. DF-ExpEnse leverages the multimodal modeling capabilities of the generative control policy to create an expressive and tractably evaluatable candidate set. It then utilizes an ensemble of critics to identify the action that best balances quality with high exploration interest. In fleet settings, DF-ExpEnse further enables cross-agent communication to facilitate collaborative exploration as a group. DF-ExpEnse can be seamlessly integrated with existing strategies that finetune pretrained generative control policies via reinforcement learning. We experimentally validate consistent sample-efficiency benefits through DF-ExpEnse across a variety of manipulation and locomotion tasks, compared to default finetuning and alternative action selection schemes. Project can be found at https://df-expense.github.io.

</details>


### [224] [Safe Local Navigation for Ackermann-Steered Robots in Unmapped Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19672)
*Christian Schaible,Shahin Sirouspour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部障碍物检测的安全引导控制框架，通过二次优化构建边界线并利用反馈线性化控制器实现阿克曼转向移动机器人在无全局目标环境中的安全局部导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决阿克曼转向移动机器人在无全局目标、未测绘环境中的安全局部导航问题，现有探索式规划器计算时间长且路径安全性不足。

Method: 1) 基于局部障碍物检测确定最安全航向角（沿最大开阔空间方向）；2) 通过凸二次优化求解最大化车辆-障碍物间距的左右边界线；3) 可选施加平行性约束和平滑约束；4) 利用反馈线性化控制器调节车辆与边界线距离，实现局部参考路径跟踪。

Result: 实验表明该方法相比现有探索式规划器，生成的导航路径更安全且计算时间显著缩短。

Conclusion: 所提控制框架能有效实现无全局目标环境下的安全局部导航，兼具高安全性和低计算开销，且开源代码便于应用。

Abstract: A control framework is proposed for safe local navigation of mobile robots equipped with Ackermann steering in unmapped environments where a global goal is absent. Based on local obstacle detections, the safest heading angle is determined along the direction of the largest open space ahead of the vehicle. Guided by this direction, bounding lines are constructed on the left and right sides of the vehicle to achieve obstacle separation. These bounding lines are obtained by solving a convex quadratic optimization that maximizes vehicle-to-obstacle clearance. Optionally, conditions are imposed on the bounding lines to preserve parallelism and smooth abrupt changes from prior control steps. A feedback-linearizing controller is then used to regulate the vehicle's distance from one or both bounding lines, effectively enabling tracking of a local reference path that preserves safety through obstacle clearance maximization. Open-source code is included for the application of this control scheme. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method produces safer navigation paths with significantly shorter computation times, compared to some existing exploration-based planners.

</details>


### [225] [ForEnt: A Multi-Modal Dataset for Characterizing Quadruped Robot Entrapments in Forest Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19675)
*Natapat Kirdwichai,Danesh Tarapore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForEnt是一个多模态数据集，包含在森林环境中使用Unitree Go2四足机器人收集的1.7公里穿越数据、69次陷入事件记录，旨在支持四足机器人森林陷入故障模式的研究与检测策略开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在森林环境中常因藤蔓等植被导致腿部被困、失稳倾覆，但缺乏专门的森林陷入故障数据集来研究这些失效模式。

Method: 使用Unitree Go2四足机器人在英国南安普顿Common Woodlands的8个森林场地进行11个序列、约1.7公里的穿越，收集了69次陷入事件。数据集包含时间同步的RGB-D图像、LiDAR扫描、本体感知数据和第三人称视频，并提供了带标签的传感器流。

Result: 成功收集了69次陷入事件，以及1.7公里穿越的多模态传感器数据，形成可复现基准测试的数据集。

Conclusion: ForEnt数据集通过提供标准化基准降低了开发森林环境下鲁棒四足机器人部署的门槛，支持陷入检测策略的评估。

Abstract: Legged robots are increasingly deployed in forests for ecological surveying and monitoring, yet their autonomy is often interrupted consequent to the challenges posed in traversing forest environments. Forest entrapments, for example, when a robot's legs are ensnared in vines or other vegetation, result in loss of stability and toppling. Such events not only disrupt the mission and require manual intervention, but also risk damage to the robot hardware. To address the absence of a dedicated dataset to investigate these failure modes in forest environments, we present ForEnt, a multi-modal dataset collected with the low-cost Unitree Go2 quadruped across eight forest sites in the Southampton Common Woodlands, UK. For our dataset, over approximately 1.7 km of traversals in 11 sequences were conducted, yielding 69 recorded entrapment events. ForEnt includes time-synchronized RGB-D images, LiDAR scans, proprioceptive data, and third-person video, enabling analysis of terrain factors contributing to entrapment and providing labeled sensor streams for reproducible benchmarking. By supporting the evaluation of entrapment detection strategies, ForEnt lowers the barrier to developing robust quadruped robot deployments in challenging forest environments.

</details>


### [226] [Route-Constrained Robust Fusion Estimation for MEMS/GNSS Integrated Navigation of Unmanned Ground Vehicles in GNSS Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19687)
*Jingzhi Cui,Chao Zhang,Yuliang Mao,Shaolin Lü,Dongmei Li,Huan Che,Rong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对卫星信号遮挡下无人地面车辆在结构化道路中的累积定位漂移问题，本文提出了一种鲁棒的路径约束状态估计方法，通过将历史航迹推算轨迹与高精地图中的任务路段匹配，生成伪位置观测并融入扩展卡尔曼滤波，有效抑制了卫星中断期间的误差累积，提升了定位连续性和道路级可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人地面车辆在结构化道路环境中，因GNSS信号严重遮挡导致的累积定位漂移问题。

Method: 建立历史航迹推算轨迹与高精地图中任务路段的对应关系，通过二维刚体变换估计路径参考位置，将其作为伪位置观测纳入扩展卡尔曼滤波更新框架，并引入触发控制、匹配质量验证、路径偏移补偿和单次更新修正限制等工程策略。

Result: 在长隧道、多段隧道和弯曲隧道三个代表性场景中，该方法有效抑制了卫星中断期间的误差累积，降低了最大偏差风险，提升了定位连续性和道路级可用性。

Conclusion: 所提路径约束方法能够在统一的状态估计框架中持续注入道路级约束，抑制相对于任务路径的位置偏差，并间接改善方位角估计，具有良好的工程实用性。

Abstract: To address cumulative localization drift of unmanned ground vehicles in structured road environments under severe Global Navigation Satellite System signal occlusion, this paper proposes a robust route-constrained state estimation method.
